  "text": "[severity: info] 14K τ 发现 Framing 影响正式评估\n\n---\n\n## Q1: Stage 1 item selection 是否可升级为更强 contribution？\n\n**是的，但需要精确定位。**\n\n14K 结果证明的是：vanilla 2PL 在 **unfiltered** benchmark 上无法区分 dosage（τ≈0）。但这不意味着 RRF item selection 本身是独立贡献——它意味��� **\"signal-guided item selection 是使 IRT calibration 有效的必要前提条件\"**。\n\n\"Exposure-based item selection via RRF\" 作为 Contribution 2 的定位是合适的。但建议措辞从 \"item selection\" 微调为 \"exposure-weighted item filtering\"——因为论文的实际做法不是 binary selection（选/不选），而是通过 RRF score 做 continuous weighting 或 top-K filtering。\n\n**不应该升级太过**的原因：\n- S1 (embedding similarity) 仍主导 RRF\n- Item selection 本身不是方法创新（任何 threshold 都行）——创新在于 \"连接 detection signal 到 IRT measurement framework\" 的 bridge\n\n**建议 Contribution 2 措辞**：\n> \"We show that signal-guided item filtering is a necessary preprocessing step for IRT-based calibration: without exposure-informed selection, vanilla 2PL on full benchmarks cannot separate contamination effects (τ≈0 on 14K MMLU). RRF fusion of S1/S2/S4 provides this filter; in practice S1 dominates.\"\n\n---\n\n## Q2: 论文 story 是否更强？\n\n**明确更强，且更诚实。**\n\n| 维度 | 原版 | 修正版 |\n|------|------|--------|\n| Claim | \"vanilla 2PL alone suffices\" | \"RRF filtering + vanilla 2PL suffices; γ optional\" |\n| 诚实度 | 隐含了 300-item pre-selection | 明确 Stage 1 的必要性 |\n| 贡献结构 | C1(θ) strong, C2(RRF) marginal | C1(θ)+C2(RRF) synergistic |\n| 审稿人攻击面 | \"如果 vanilla 2PL 够了为什么需要 ContamCal?\" | 已回答：因为需要 item selection |\n| 方法完整性 | 2-stage pipeline 但 Stage 1 看起来 optional | 两个 stage 都有实证必要性 |\n\n修正后的 story 回答了一个隐含但重要的问题：\"如果 θ 就够了，为什么需要 detection signals？\" 答案：signals 不是为了 γ（γ is optional diagnostic），而是为了 **指导 item selection** 使 θ estimation 聚焦在 contamination-relevant items 上。\n\n---\n\n## Q3: 暴露的弱点\n\n**三个攻击面，均可防御：**\n\n### 3a. Item selection 阈值敏感性\n- 审��人必问：\"300 items 是怎么选的？换 threshold 结果变吗？\"\n- W10 sensitivity analysis 正是为此准备。如果 τ 在 200-500 items 范围内稳定，则 robust；如果剧烈变化则需讨论。\n- **防御**：Section 5.1 或 Appendix 加 sensitivity curve。\n\n### 3b. 2-chain MCMC 可靠性\n- 论文如需引用 14K τ=0.024 这个数字：\n  - 如果作为 \"τ ≈ 0\"（定性结论）→ 2 chains 足够，Rhat < 1.003\n  - 如果作为精确数值 → 应标注 \"preliminary (2/4 chains completed before server interruption)\" 或重跑\n- **建议**：论文中用定性表述 \"τ is indistinguishable from zero on the full benchmark\" 而非精确报数\n\n### 3c. Circular reasoning concern\n- 审稿人可能质疑：\"你用 contamination signals 选 items，然后证明这些 items 上能检测 contamination——这不是循环论证吗？\"\n- **防御关键**：Oracle ablation (C6) 已证明即使用 ground-truth exposure，γ 仍不显著。所以 τ=0.638 的来源���是\"选了 contaminated items 然后 detect contamination\"，而是\"IRT θ 在 signal-selected items 上能分离 scale-level ability\"。\n- 这个防御论证应该写进论文。\n\n---\n\n## Q4: 进一步利用机会\n\n### 4a. Item selection proportion 曲线（强烈推荐）\n\n展示 τ vs item-selection-ratio 曲线（e.g., top 100/200/300/500/1000/all 14042 items by RRF score）。\n\n- 如果存在 \"elbow\"（如 τ 在 top-200 开始��和），这本身就是一个 finding\n- ���据需求：已有 full 14K per-item RRF scores + per-item responses；只需按不同 threshold 切分后重新计算 θ→τ（纯 CPU，每个 threshold ~5 min Stan fit on 300-1000 items）\n- 这可以成为一张很好的 Appendix figure\n\n### 4b. 与 Oracle ablation 的结合��事\n\n将 14K τ≈0 + Oracle γ not significant + 300-item τ=0.64 串成一个完整论证：\n\n1. Full benchmark: vanilla 2PL 无法区分 dosage（τ≈0）→ 需要 item selection\n2. 300-item RRF-selected: vanilla 2PL 成功（τ=0.64）→ RRF Stage 1 有效\n3. Oracle on 300-item: γ 仍不显著 → γ 的不显著不是 signal 问题而是 J 问题\n4. 结论：**RRF 为 θ 提供了有效的 item focus；γ 需要 J≥50 才有 power**\n\n这是一个完整的、逻辑自洽的 evidence chain。建议作为 §5.1 的核心叙事结构。\n\n---\n\n## 综合建议\n\n1. **Contribution 2 升级**：从 \"marginal\" 到 \"synergistically necessary\"，用 14K 对比作为直接证据\n2. **§5.1 加 full-benchmark contrast paragraph**：一段即可，展示 τ 从 0.64 到 ≈0 的对比\n3. **Appendix 加 item-proportion sensitivity curve**（如果 W10 或新实验提供数据）\n4. **Discussion 加 circular reasoning 防御**（结合 Oracle ablation）\n5. **14K τ 用定性而非精确数值报告**（2 chains preliminary）\n6. 服务器恢复后优先重跑 4-chain 版本以获得可正式���用的数字",

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   I am the independent Research Reviewer for the contamcal-irt-calibration project (Loop continuation). My ongoing tasks:
   - Audit worker outputs as they complete (J=16 MCMC, eval, tau computation, oracle ablation, CI coverage, prior check)
   - Verify paper framing consistency after Round 3 revision (batch 1 C1+C5+W7+W8+C2 fixes)
   - Assess Action Matrix for Round 3 reviews (2.5/5 score, 8 critical + 10 warning)
   - Evaluate framing implications of the 14K full-MMLU τ≈0 discovery
   - Monitor GPU compliance and server status
   - Post findings to Director via `reviewer_post_to_director`

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Item Response Theory (IRT) 2PL+γ model for contamination calibration
   - Stan MCMC (CmdStan, 4 chains, 1000 warmup + 1000 samples, adapt_delta=0.95)
   - Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF) for signal aggregation (S1 embedding, S2 Min-K%++, S4 Self-Critique)
   - J=13 respondent pool (8 Qwen 1.5B + 5 Qwen 3B, excluding 0.5B position bias)
   - J=16 cross-family pool (8 Qwen 1.5B + 3 Llama 3B [d000/d005/d025] + 5 Qwen 3B)
   - Cross-family v5: MMLU J=18, GSM8K J=28, ARC J=20 (different compositions per benchmark)
   - Posterior Predictive P-value (PPP): acceptable range [0.1, 0.9], PPP≈0.5 ideal
   - Kendall τ for rank preservation, MAE for calibration quality
   - Oracle exposure ablation: binary item-level e_{ij} = 1{item_i ∈ training_set_j}
   - Shrinkage = 1 - (posterior_var / prior_var) for prior domination check
   - Full MMLU 14K τ≈0 vs 300-item RRF-selected τ=0.638: proves Stage 1 necessity
   - SeetaCloud container interruption ("Operation not permitted" killing all 4 chains simultaneously)
   - EMNLP 2026 ARR submission (D129 pivot: "contamination detection" → "calibration decomposition")

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `/root/autodl-tmp/exp006/mmlu_domain_irt_j16_cross_family/*/irt_results.json`
     - J=16 cross-family domain results (8 domains completed)
     - STEM_Math: γ=-0.386, PPP=0.998; Humanities: γ=-0.947 (significant), PPP=1.000; Social_Sciences: γ=-1.092 (significant), PPP=1.000
   
   - `/root/autodl-tmp/exp006/mmlu_domain_irt_no05B/*/irt_results.json`
     - J=13 Qwen-only domain results for comparison
     - STEM_Math: γ=+0.499, PPP=0.206; STEM_CS_Eng: γ=+0.497, PPP=0.392
   
   - `/root/autodl-tmp/exp006/cross_family_v5/gsm8k/irt_results.json`
     - J=28, 300 items, γ=-0.131 (not significant), PPP=0.842, raw_MAE=0.179, cal_MAE=0.008 (96% reduction)
   
   - `/root/autodl-tmp/exp006/cross_family_v5/arc_challenge/irt_results.json`
     - J=20, 300 items, γ=+0.007 (essentially zero), PPP=0.435, raw_MAE=0.070, cal_MAE=0.015 (78% reduction)
   
   - `/root/autodl-tmp/exp006/oracle_exposure_ablation/`

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Worker PPP misinterpretation**: Worker called PPP=0.998 "excellent fit" — WRONG. PPP near 1.0 indicates model misspecification. Flagged in Director report.
   - **Worker γ direction error**: Worker claimed "γ<0 consistent with contamination hypothesis" — WRONG. γ>0 is contamination direction (contaminated items easier). γ<0 means contaminated items are harder (opposite). Flagged as separate warning to Director.
   - **C6 Oracle ablation design flaw**: Director proposed e_{ij}=dosage/100 which is constant per model (unidentifiable, confounded with θ). I flagged this — correct design is binary item-level indicator. Agent used correct design in actual execution.
   - **Registry not updated for exp_008**: Worker claimed registry updated to DONE but it remained RUNNING. Noted as operational issue.
   - **SeetaCloud container interruption**: All running CPU processes killed (~88 min into execution). Root cause: container platform policy, not OOM or code bug. Fix: rerun with --skip-pooled, split long tasks.
   - **MMLU full-item tau killed**: 2/4 chains completed before server died. Worker extracted results from 2 completed chains (Rhat acceptable at <1.003).
   - **Cross-family table uses v4 data**: Paper Table 5 shows MMLU γ=+0.41 (v4) instead of +0.35 (v5 with s1 bug fix). Flagged for update.
   - **CI coverage numbers outdated in Limitations**: Paper says "50% CI ~35%" but latest computation shows 45% (ARC) and 83% (MMLU). Flagged for update.
   - **ARC γ=-1.46 significant despite PPP=0.027**: Paper reports significant ARC γ from a misspecified model without disclosing PPP issue. Flagged as warning.

5. Problem Solving:
   - **14K vs 300-item τ discrepancy resolved**: Full MMLU τ≈0 because 98% of items are clean, diluting signal. 300-item RRF-selected τ=0.638 because selection focuses on contamination-relevant items. This proves Stage 1 (RRF item selection) is necessary for IRT calibration to work on τ metric.
   - **J=16 PPP degradation explained**: 2PL+γ model is structurally inadequate for cross-family pooling. Llama wasn't trained on same contamination data, so "contaminated items" (per Qwen-derived signals) aren't easier for Llama, pushing γ negative and PPP toward 1.0.
   - **Oracle ablation interpretation**: Even perfect exposure labels don't make γ significant at J=13 → bottleneck is sample size (J), not signal quality.
   - **Prior domination ruled out**: Shrinkage 0.78-0.94 confirms data contributes 78-94% of information. STEM γ≈0.5 uniformity at J=13 is statistical power limitation, not prior influence.
   - **Paper framing successfully verified**: D129 pivot to "calibration decomposition" is consistently implemented across all sections after batch 1 fixes.

        "content": "{\n  \"raw\": \"服务器现在不可达，这正好印证了诊断结果。\\n\\n根因已明确。日志完整分析：\\n\\n1. **进程正常运行了 ~88 分钟**（20:07 → 21:35），domain-level IRT 全部完成\\n2. **进入 pooled fit**（2000 items, 4 chains, max_depth=12），采样到 60% 时\\n3. **所有 4 条 MCMC chain 在同一秒被 kill**：`code '1' Operation not permitted`（21:35:42）\\n4. **服务器当前也不可达**（验证了容器不稳定）\\n\\n```json\\n{\\\"root_cause\\\":\\\"SeetaCloud容器被平台强制停止/迁移，导致CmdStan 4条采样链同时收到 EPERM (21:35:42 所有chain同时 'Operation not permitted')。pooled fit (2000 items, J=16, max_depth=12) 耗时长，暴露在容器中断窗口内。附加问题: --skip-pooled 未生效，pooled fit 不应该被执行\\\",\\\"error_type\\\":\\\"infrastructure\\\",\\\"fix_steps\\\":[\\\"确认 SeetaCloud 容器实例运行时间限制（可能有 idle timeout 或计费停机）\\\",\\\"重启容器后重新提交，确保传入 --skip-pooled 参数（当前 log 显示 pooled fit 仍在执行）\\\",\\\"如需 pooled fit：减少 items 数（当前 2000）或降低 num_warmup/num_samples，缩短单次 MCMC 运行时间至 <3 分钟\\\",\\\"添加增量保存：domain fit 完成后立即 dump all_domain_results.json，pooled 单独跑\\\"],\\\"can_auto_fix\\\":false,\\\"affects_paper\\\":false}\\n```\\n\\n**总结**：不是代码 bug，是 SeetaCloud 平台中断了容器。两次实验（parent 和 theta extraction）都在同一模式下失败。等容器恢复后，带 `--skip-pooled` 重跑即可完成 domain-level 结果；pooled fit 需要单独短时运行或拆分。\",\n  \"data\": {\n    \"root_cause\": \"SeetaCloud容器被平台强制停止/迁移，导致CmdStan 4条采样链同时收到 EPERM (21:35:42 所有chain同时 'Operation not permitted')。pooled fit (2000 items, J=16, max_depth=12) 耗时长，暴露在容器中断窗口内。附加问题: --skip-pooled 未生效，pooled fit 不应该被执行\",\n    \"error_type\": \"infrastructure\",\n    \"fix_steps\": [\n      \"确认 SeetaCloud 容器实例运行时间限制（可能有 idle timeout 或计费停机）\",\n      \"重启容器后重新提交，确保传入 --skip-pooled 参数（当前 log 显示 pooled fit 仍在执行）\",\n      \"如需 pooled fit：减少 items 数（当前 2000）或降低 num_warmup/num_samples，缩短单次 MCMC 运行时间至 <3 分钟\",\n      \"添加增量保存：domain fit 完成后立即 dump all_domain_results.json，pooled 单独跑\"\n    ],\n    \"can_auto_fix\": false,\n    \"affects_paper\": false\n  }\n}",

        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_13/projects/contamcal-irt-calibration/docs/paper/conclusion.tex",
        "content": "\\section{Discussion and Conclusion}\\label{sec:conclusion}\n\nWe investigated whether IRT can separate ability from contamination artifacts on LLM benchmarks, finding three results: (1)~IRT ability estimation provides the dominant calibration benefit independent of $\\gamma$ (53\\% MAE reduction, $J=13$); (2)~Min-K\\%++ exhibits directional reversal at small scales; (3)~domain-level $\\gamma$ is exploratory at $J=13$, requiring $J \\geq 50$ for confirmation.\n\n\\paragraph{When not to use ContamCal.}\nApplying non-significant $\\gamma$ produces overcorrection (11$\\times$ on GSM8K); under heterogeneous effects the additive $\\gamma$ averages toward zero even with perfect labels. ARC's marginal fit (PPP$=$0.03) adds a caution that significant $\\gamma$ may reflect specification error.\n\n\\paragraph{Implications and future work.}\nIRT-based calibration should be standard infrastructure (53\\% MAE reduction without detection); Min-K\\%++ should not be trusted below 1.5B. Scaling to $J \\geq 50$ and nonlinear specifications are natural next steps.\n",

Let me assess the current state. The server is down (SeetaCloud container interrupted), and the most recent worker results I've been reviewing show:

1. **Full MMLU 14K τ result** (2 chains): τ=0.0237, p=0.893 — essentially zero correlation between theta and dosage on full MMLU. This contrasts sharply with the 300-item RRF-selected τ=0.6377 (p=0.0061).

2. **J=16 theta 5 domains check**: Failed — 0/5 domains got theta extracted. 

Only the first three domains (STEM_Math, STEM_Science, STEM_CS_Eng) have theta values; Medical completed its MCMC sampling but the container was killed before writing theta, likely due to out-of-memory issues. The full-item tau check also got interrupted mid-sampling with chains at varying completion levels before the container restarted. The J=16 theta extraction failure wasn't a code bug—all four chains hit permission errors simultaneously at 21:35:42, confirming the container instability. I can see the current discussion section in Conclusion.tex.

The 14K τ framing assessment was sent to the Director, so now I'm waiting for server recovery and checking if any new worker results have come in. Looking at the conclusion.tex content, the key findings about IRT ability estimation providing 53% MAE reduction, Min-K%++ directional reversal at small scales, and domain-level γ requiring J≥50 for confirmation all align with what we've observed.

However, I'm noticing some gaps — the conclusion doesn't capture the 14K τ≈0 finding or the full-item versus selected-item τ discrepancy that really drives home the Stage 1 necessity argument. It also misses the cross-family preliminary results and the Oracle ablation insight about the J bottleneck. Since the paper is still in revision with more batches pending, this is more of a note for the next round of updates rather than something that needs immediate fixing.

Now I should check what's actually actionable right now. The server is down so there's no active experiment work to monitor. Let me review the conclusion for any issues, see if there are new worker results to process, and figure out when to schedule the next check.

Looking more carefully at the conclusion text, the core claims seem solid — IRT ability estimation gives the dominant calibration benefit, Min-K%++ shows directional reversal at small scales, and domain-level γ needs more data to confirm. But I'm getting stuck on this "11× overcorrection" claim about GSM8K. The actual numbers from the data show a 96% MAE reduction when γ is applied, which is the opposite of overcorrection. I need to figure out what comparison this is actually referring to — maybe it's about the magnitude of the correction relative to something else, or perhaps it's describing a different scenario where γ is applied inappropriately.

Since the server's down anyway, let me use this time to dig into the conclusion more systematically and verify whether the paper's current framing aligns with the 14K τ assessment I just completed.
